practically undiscoverable, or disables consequential uptake of nonviolent,
evidence-bearing correction or opposition that does not itself violate a
specifiable reciprocal right. It most directly detects a fault in the correction
architecture. The strict program diagnosis still requires all seven gates at one
specified unit and period.

The observation contributes differently to each gate:

| Ideology gate | Evidential contribution of the censorship sentinel |
|---|---|
| **1. Bearer** | Identifies candidate enforcing actors, offices, platforms, or structures, but does not by itself reconstruct the Culture Program whose authority they carry. |
| **2. Generator** | Recurrent use of a fitted grammar—protected object or emergency, moral authorization, exceptional control, opposition-as-complicity, and renewed power after resistance—can support a generator hypothesis. One prohibition is insufficient. |
| **3. Operative un-reality** | Supported when an identified authorized account continues to govern fact, guilt, standing, permission, sanction, remedy, or jurisdiction through the disabled correction. Censorship can also occur before un-reality has been successfully installed. |
| **4. Correction closure** | The strongest direct relation: relevant contradiction is prevented from becoming consequential correction, or a sustained correction is denied reception, answer, adjudication, uptake, or repair. |
| **5. Un-reasoned defence** | Requires additional reason-shaped immunization. Criticism must be converted through asymmetric concepts, burdens, exceptions, or causal accounts into proof of danger, contamination, disinformation, hostility to the protected object, or need for more control. Naked silencing alone does not satisfy the gate. |
| **6. Installation and reproduction** | Supported when law, procedure, platforms, records, incentives, trusted carriers, institutions, and private proxies recurrently enforce the exclusion and preserve the protected operation. |
| **7. Collective scale** | Supported when the mechanism is durable across a solidarity-bearing organization, population, or jurisdiction rather than confined to an isolated dispute. The number of persons technically reachable is evidence of scope, not proof of the complete program. |

The inference is Bayesian and staged rather than deductive:

```text
censorship of relevant correction
→ strong evidence of correction-protecting malfunction
→ Ideology / un-reality / un-reason / systemic-lying audit
≠ automatic gate completion or equal probability for every diagnosis
```

If the protected account remains operative through recurrent, fitted,
multi-channel suppression, the same observation becomes increasingly direct
evidence of organized un-reality and movement toward systemic lying. If
articulate rationalization makes the correction confirm the account it
challenges, it additionally supports un-reason. If power simply silences
opposition, the operation can remain naked domination or another non-inferential
preservation mechanism without satisfying strict Ideology.

The gate concerns operative correction rather than universal propositional
falsity. A representation can be held un-really even if it happens to be true
when its authority has been made incapable of losing; a false claim can remain
ordinary corrigible error. Ideology therefore does not mean that every contained
proposition is false, every carrier knowingly lies, or one conscious designer
coordinates the formation.

At the same specified program unit, the closure required by Ideology places its
load-bearing protected account on the **Pure-Ideology** side of the branch test.
The converse does not hold. A bounded religious program can retain an
extra-empirical sacred promise beyond accessible defeat while neither generating
social un-reality nor recruiting un-reason to defend worldly practical
jurisdiction. Whole-program classification must also respect scale: an Ideology
component inside a mixed Culture Program Complex does not classify every
component or carrier.

#### Systemic Ideology and systemic lying

A **Systemic Ideology** is an Ideology whose generative and correction-closing
operation has become population-scale, cross-domain, and constitutional: it
recurrently determines what may count as social reality across the relevant
common order. The functional threshold is not raw headcount. Ask whether the
program:

- reproduces beyond direct acquaintance with its current advocates;
- possesses impersonal carrier roles, transmission, memory, and succession;
- governs consequential standing or conduct for a population or jurisdiction;
- survives turnover among individual carriers; and
- recurs across enough linked domains that the pattern is not one local error.

Systemic Ideology is one important candidate route into the
[systemic-lying epistemic constitution](0030-epistemic-constitution.semantic-definition.md#systemic-lying-epistemic-constitution),
but the classifiers remain distinct. Systemic lying is the constitutional
meta-order that recurrently generates, selects, authorizes, protects, and
replaces organized un-reality because independent truth has become subordinate
to usefulness, loyalty, control, or authority. It can employ mechanisms other
than un-reason, while localized Ideology need not constitute a whole
constitutional meta-order. Test domain reach, recurrent replacement, public
avowal, records, correction, and practical jurisdiction separately.

#### Minogue-ideology: the oppression--revelation--liberation subtype

**Minogue-ideology** is the narrower subtype whose ideological generator joins:

```text
particular grievance or limitation
→ hidden oppressive system
→ oppressed and oppressor positions
→ ordinary non-recognition explained as false consciousness
→ revelation, awakening, or corrected sight
→ struggle and mobilization
→ acquisition of transformative power
→ liberation from the structure
→ terminal harmony with comparatively underspecified institutions
→ criticism exposed to classification as revisionism
```

The subtype therefore requires more than an oppression word or an adverse
structural explanation. Preserve Minogue's hidden comprehensive structure,
asymmetric consciousness, salvational revelation, mobilization, power,
liberating terminus, and anti-political treatment of ordinary counterjudgment.
The detailed source reconstruction, the costly-formation/effortless-perception
relation, the clear-pond terminus, the distinction between pure type and actual
movements, and his two separate terminal judgments are owned by
[*Mythology Construction*](0040-mythology-construction.semantic-definition.md#minogue-ideology-the-narrower-subtype).

#### Ideology as content axis; Fascism as form axis

Ideology classifies executable semantic--epistemic **content**: generative
grammar, authorized social world, and correction defence as embodied in a
formation. Fascism classifies **morphological form**: the strong, whole-polity,
jealous, subjecthood-absorptive realization of mechanical solidarity with an
exclusive directing claim to incarnate, interpret, and act for the collective
self.

```text
CONTENT AXIS                       FORM AXIS
Ideology / no Ideology             Fascism / no Fascism
```

The axes are analytically distinct but operationally coupled. Ideology can be
installed in a non-Fascist form. Fascism can operate through openly avowed
domination without satisfying the Ideology gate. A formation can satisfy both,
but neither classifier may be inferred from the other. The functional morphology
of mechanical solidarity remains the generic whole-formation description
available to Culture Programs generally; it is not a downstream layer or an
Ideology-specific discriminator.

### 4.6 Essence-based classification and heterogeneous contents

The branches are essence-based ideal types that permit predominant
whole-program classification. The classification is categorical at the proposed
centre of gravity; evidence about that centre can remain uncertain.

A Culture Strategy may contain:

- nonfalsifiable ultimate values;
- rituals;
- sacred symbols;
- inherited loyalty;
- inaccurate auxiliary beliefs.

A Pure-Ideology Culture Program may contain:

- effective agricultural practice;
- durable charity;
- accurate empirical claims;
- prudent legal procedures;
- or a reality-answerable organizational strategy.

Whole-program classification must not silently transfer to every component.
Where several fitted packages form a large object, use **Culture Program
Complex**, identify the load-bearing centre, and report internal heterogeneity.

## 5. Canonical hierarchy and familiar forms

The successor hierarchy preserves the centre-of-gravity intention of the legacy
diagram:

```text
Culture Program
├── Pure-Ideology Culture Program
│   ├── Ideology
│   │   └── Minogue-ideology
│   ├── Cultural Essentialism
│   ├── Religion
│   │   └── Sect
│   │       └── Cult
│   ├── Identity / Mentality
│   │   └── Intersectionality
│   └── Personality Cult
└── Culture Strategy
    ├── Business Strategy
    ├── Political Strategy
    ├── Culture-Embedded Strategy
    └── Strategic Worldview

Cross-cutting morphological form axis:
Fascism / no Fascism
```

This is a theoretical classification proposal, not a lexical claim that every
ordinary use of these words has the same essence. The placement of Ideology
under Pure-Ideology applies at the same specified program unit because the strict
Ideology gate requires load-bearing correction closure. Fascism is deliberately
outside the inheritance tree because it asks a different morphological question.

### 5.1 Subtractive-essence hypotheses

| Familiar form | Candidate load-bearing essence | Boundary warning |
|---|---|---|
| **Ideology** | Sufficiently installed generator of authorized social un-reality whose operative authority is defended through un-reason | Doctrine, false belief, one un-real representation, one episode of un-reason, or Pure-Ideology finality alone is insufficient; require all seven gates |
| **Minogue-ideology** | Ideology whose generator joins hidden oppression, opposed positions, asymmetric consciousness, revelation, mobilization, transformative power, liberation, and a terminal anti-political ideal | One oppression claim, structural explanation, awakening metaphor, or political movement is insufficient; preserve the pure-type/actual-movement distinction |
| **Religion** | Sacred or transcendent relation protected from accessible defeat | A religious institution can contain many reality-answerable practices and strategies |
| **Sect** | More bounded and often more demanding religious descendant | Smallness, novelty, or unpopularity alone is insufficient |
| **Cult** | Highly concentrated devotion, authority, or demanded attachment | The ordinary term is pejorative and needs operational criteria |
| **Personality Cult** | Affective loyalty to a person protected against ordinary performance defeat | Admiration, electoral support, or charismatic leadership alone is insufficient |
| **Cultural Essentialism** | Morally weighted in-group/out-group identity treated as an enduring collective essence | Cultural continuity and group association are not by themselves essentialism |
| **Identity / Mentality** | Identity-bearing classification that supplies final evaluative belonging | Identity also participates in Culture Strategies and mixed braids |
| **Intersectionality** | Identity classification and moral standing organized through intersecting group positions | Analytical use of interacting variables is not automatically a Pure-Ideology program |
| **Fascism** | Strong, whole-polity, jealous, and subjecthood-absorptive functional morphology of mechanical solidarity in which a centralized or distributed directing function claims exclusive authority to incarnate, interpret, and act for the collective self | The complete conjunction must be evidenced; solidarity, hierarchy, coercion, moral closure, communist or anti-fascist self-description, and hostile accusation are each insufficient alone |
| **Business Strategy** | Coordinated causal wager expected to accumulate organizational or market value | Profit, fashion, or executive assertion does not establish causal adequacy |
| **Political Strategy** | Causal wager aimed at observable political or institutional consequences | A strategy can carry Pure-Ideology ends or be embedded in such a program |
| **Culture-Embedded Strategy** | Productive mechanism installed as norm, virtue, duty, custom, feeling, role, and practice | A norm's popularity or moral warmth does not establish material payoff |
| **Strategic Worldview** | General causal orientation answerable to worldly consequences | A total worldview protected from defeat belongs on the other branch |

The table retains Fascism for comparison with familiar forms, but its row is a
cross-cutting morphological classifier rather than a Pure-Ideology descendant.
The hierarchy allows redundant or multiple-looking legacy relations where the
subtractive essence warrants them. Diagram neatness must not decide ontology.

#### Cross-doctrinal Fascism and the self-exemption warning

The canonical [whole-polity definition of
Fascism](0020-epistemic-agency.semantic-definition.md#fascism-as-a-strong-whole-polity-realization)
is deliberately independent of the collective self's substantive name and of a
modern state's existence. Nation, racialized *Volk*, proletariat, revolutionary
people, religious body, empire, or another collective subject can occupy the
collective-self position; chief, council, party, priesthood, state, or distributed
apparatus can occupy the directing position.

> **Do not exempt a formation from the Fascism classification because it calls
> itself communist, socialist, religious, anti-fascist, emancipatory, or hostile
> to a recognized historical fascist enemy. If the complete Culture Mechanics
> conjunction is evidenced, classify the formation by its operation.**

This rule also runs in the other direction. An official or hostile use of
*fascist* is not evidence that the target satisfies the type. State the unit and
period, test every component, and preserve counterevidence. The project category
therefore resists both **self-exemption** and **enemy-label inflation**.

This is one application of the [strong Durkheimian accountability and symmetry
rule](0040-mythology-construction.semantic-definition.md#strong-durkheimian-accountability-and-symmetry).
Every Culture Program is judged by the same stated-doctrine, installed-operation,
offspring, consequence, causality, and correction tests. A benevolent semantic
wrapper cannot defeat recurrent operational evidence; destructive consequences
cannot establish program responsibility without a causal relation. There is no
doctrinal-family exemption in either direction.

<a id="polity-scale-gleichschaltung-hazard"></a>

#### Polity-scale *Gleichschaltung* hazard: process before result {#polity-scale-gleichschaltung-hazard}

**Functional morphology of mechanical solidarity** names a resulting social
configuration. ***Gleichschaltung*** names a transformation process that may be
attempting to produce or intensify such a configuration:

```text
attempted polity-scale Gleichschaltung
        ≠ completed mechanical-solidarity morphology
        ≠ completed Fascism conjunction
        → strong process-level warning that such a formation is being built
```

The historical term came from electrical engineering. In Nazi usage it named
the alignment of political, social, economic, and cultural institutions with the
NSDAP and the loss, dissolution, or replacement of independent organizations.
The German Federal Agency for Civic Education notes both the term's
[electrotechnical origin and the destruction of organizational
independence](https://www.bpb.de/kurz-knapp/lexika/das-junge-politik-lexikon/320425/gleichschaltung/).
The German Historical Museum describes the replacement of Weimar's plural
institutional diversity by adaptation to NSDAP objectives, including both forced
and voluntary accommodation, and records incomplete resistance in parts of the
churches ([DHM](https://www.dhm.de/lemo/kapitel/ns-regime/etablierung/gleichschaltung)).
The United States Holocaust Memorial Museum documents the process across
[economic, social, cultural, associational, media, youth, and leisure
domains](https://encyclopedia.ushmm.org/content/en/article/gleichschaltung-coordinating-the-nazi-state).
The historical sources establish the Nazi process; the diagnostic below and its
field mapping are Culture Mechanics developments.

> **Polity-scale *Gleichschaltung* hazard is an attempted cross-domain alignment
> of independent institutions, human carriers, symbolic and conformity-marker
> environments, communication channels, and institutional-operational systems
> toward one privileged ideological direction or authority-addressed pole. Its
> appearance is strong early evidence that a functional morphology of
> polity-scale mechanical solidarity is being constructed, even though the
> resulting formation is not yet complete and a complete Culture Program may not
> yet be reconstructable.**

The diagnostic threshold concerns a specified polity or population and requires
more than one centralized decision, common technical standard, emergency order,
public campaign, or instance of conformity. Test the conjunction:

1. **Population or polity ambition:** the project addresses the encompassing
   society or expands toward population-wide jurisdiction rather than remaining
   one bounded voluntary association.
2. **Cross-domain alignment:** multiple formerly independent domains—such as
   government, administration, law, media, education, professions, associations,
   labour, religion, culture, or leisure—are being aligned in one direction.
3. **Privileged ideological orientation:** the same authority, collective-subject
   claim, ideological direction, or Culture Program is made the privileged pole
   of allegiance, identity, interpretation, sentiment, and service, rather than
   merely supplying interoperability or one bounded common output. The common
   direction can be evidenced before its full doctrine or parent program is
   known.
4. **Loss of independent orientation:** organizations, offices, and trusted
   carrier positions are dissolved, replaced, captured, subordinated, or made
   dependent upon appointments, permissions, finance, sanctions, or correction
   routes controlled by authorities already aligned with the privileged
   direction.
5. **Constriction of plural correction:** dissent becomes disloyalty, alternative
   authorities lose standing, and refusal, association, appeal, correction, or
   effective exit becomes harder.

The diagnostic must inspect the **whole installation**, not only explicit
doctrine or population-facing interpretive authorities. The established
[systemic-skeleton/living-cultural-body
anatomy](#systemic-skeleton-and-living-cultural-body) can be unfolded into
three observation planes plus their coupling:

| Observation plane | What to inspect | Diagnostic question |
|---|---|---|
| **Flesh-and-blood carriers** | Leaders, officials, teachers, clergy, journalists, professionals, organizers, trusted opinion leaders, appointments, removals, formation, scripts, rewards, sanctions, and peer enforcement | Are independently answerable persons and roles being selected, trained, routed, or disciplined toward the same privileged direction? |
| **Symbols and conformity markers** | Flags, emblems, uniforms, insignia, salutes, slogans, rituals, naming and renaming, monuments, public displays, badges, prescribed formulae, and other markers of visible alignment | Do symbols merely identify and express, or are their display, repetition, interpretation, or refusal becoming tests of loyalty, access, standing, safety, or legitimacy? |
| **Institutional-operational systems—the systemic skeleton** | Law, offices, jurisdictions, procedures, curricula, media governance, finance, credentials, permissions, surveillance, sanctions, records, exception mechanisms, review, appeal, correction, and succession | Are systems being rewired so that the same direction governs appointments, resources, permissible decisions, exceptions, and correction across domains? |
| **Cross-plane coupling** | Recurrence of the same direction through people, symbols, and systems, including feedback among public display, local enforcement, institutional action, and the directing function | Do the planes independently vary, or do they reinforce one another as one alignment process? |

Inventory the first three planes independently before judging their coupling.
Do not redescribe a flag as doctrine, a human carrier as a system, or an
institutional topology as a belief. Symbols and systems are diagnostic objects in
their own right, not merely illustrations of a Culture Program inferred in
advance; their common direction is the later relational finding.

This is a **morphological diagnosis before complete program attribution**. The
analyst may identify a common alignment direction from convergent appointments,
symbols, markers, rules, permissions, sanctions, and blocked correction without
yet possessing a defensible reconstruction of one coherent Culture Program or
its genealogy. After identifying that direction, separately test whether it
constitutes or serves a Culture Program, which content is load-bearing, who or
what directs it, and which actors or structures bear responsibility.

A Fraenkelian ***Maßnahmenstaat*** or *Doppelstaat* can be relevant system-plane
evidence where politically classified matters are withdrawn from ordinary legal
guarantees and the prerogative component helps enforce the common direction. It
is neither required nor sufficient for *Gleichschaltung*. Preserve the separate
Fraenkelian gate: one emergency measure, discretionary act, or bounded and
reviewable exception does not establish a *Maßnahmenstaat*, and a
*Maßnahmenstaat* finding does not by itself establish the remaining cross-domain,
human, symbolic, and corrective relations of this diagnostic.

Rhetoric or aspiration alone is a lead. One flag, emblem, uniform, ritual,
conformity marker, common information system, law, office, or exceptional measure
is likewise insufficient. Concrete and convergent cross-domain acts can cross
the hazard threshold before alignment succeeds everywhere. The signal initially
attaches to the process, privileged direction, directing function, and relevant
carrying and institutional structures under examination—not automatically to a
reconstructed Culture Program, every participant, or one hidden operator.
Program attribution, actor-specific accusation, conspiracy, completed Fascism,
collective fusion, and person-directed sanction each require their own evidence
and process.

The process can combine imposition from above with ***Selbstgleichschaltung***:
institutions and carriers may anticipate the emerging field, voluntarily adapt,
compete to display conformity, and amplify the direction without waiting for an
individual command. The German Historical Museum describes Nazi transformation
as also a society's
[*Selbstgleichschaltung*](https://www.dhm.de/archiv/ausstellungen/hitler-und-die-deutschen/die_deutsche_gesellschaft_und_hitler.html).
This supports the field model's reciprocal rather than purely external reading:
bottom-up alignment can return recognition and gain to the directing function.
It does not make coerced population-scale alignment voluntary or prove one plan.

##### Voluntary thick community and plural society

The diagnostic is jurisdiction-sensitive. Culture Mechanics uses Tönnies's
[*Gemeinschaft und Gesellschaft*](https://archive.org/details/gemeinschaftundg00tn)
as a source vocabulary for the contrast, but the following constitutional
boundary is project-developed rather than Tönnies's own diagnostic:

| | **Voluntary thick community (*Gemeinschaft*)** | **Plural society (*Gesellschaft*)** |
|---|---|---|
| **Typical unit** | Convent, monastery, intentional order, association, or another bounded solidarity-bearing community | Encompassing polity containing persons and many legitimate communities, associations, programmes, and ends |
| **Telos and *Beseelung*** | Adults may explicitly choose a thick common telos and practices intended to deepen *Beseelung* | The common shell protects coexistence; it does not install one comprehensive telos in the population |
| **Membership and exit** | Entry is voluntary and exit is effective, safe, and materially real rather than merely formal | Membership is largely unchosen and population-level exit is unusually costly, unavailable, or cannot substitute for internal freedom |
| **Jurisdiction** | Bounded internally, subject to common rights and law, without compulsory claims over outsiders or captive dependants | Holds coercive common jurisdiction and therefore owes standing, plural association, refusal, appeal, and correction to differently oriented persons |
| **Diagnostic meaning** | Strong local mechanical solidarity is not by itself polity-scale *Gleichschaltung*; coercion, captive membership, abuse, or subjecthood absorption remain separately diagnosable | Cross-domain human, symbolic, and systemic alignment toward one privileged ideological direction is the polity-scale *Gleichschaltung* hazard; complete Culture Program attribution may follow later |

A plural society may contain many voluntary thick communities. It ceases to act
pluralistically when it converts the common shell into one compulsory thick
community. This yields the strict constitutional boundary:

> **There is no pluralist *Gleichschaltung*. If coordination is narrow,
> function-specific, bounded, corrigible, temporary, and leaves independent
> association and belief intact, it is not *Gleichschaltung* in this diagnostic
> sense. If it satisfies the process gate, individualistic, humanist, democratic,
> emancipatory, or pluralist self-description cannot exculpate it.**

The signal justifies early scrutiny and proportionate protection of independent
institutions, trusted routes, refusal, appeal, and correction; a pluralist order
need not wait until the target morphology is complete. It does not itself justify
collective punishment, compelled counter-ideology, suppression of voluntary
thick communities, or unreviewable emergency power in the name of preventing
alignment.

<a id="historical-to-active-vocabulary-crosswalk"></a>

##### Historical-to-active vocabulary crosswalk: new names over old operations {#historical-to-active-vocabulary-crosswalk}

The controlled Loki lens asks what an attractive term **does**, which authorities
and carrying structures it joins, who may refuse, where correction survives, and
what configuration results. It does not allow the term's moral warmth to answer
those questions in advance:

> **The old operation does not need its old name. But a new name does not prove
> the old operation. Strip the label, specify the work, and apply the typed
> gate.**

This is the crosswalk's **anti-camouflage and anti-guilt-by-word rule**. A label
such as *anti-fascist*, *democratic*, *resilient*, *stakeholder*, *inclusive*, or
*whole-of-society* has zero exculpatory weight against an evidenced operation.
The same label also has zero convicting weight without that operation. Semantic
camouflage, innocent reuse, and genuinely different institutional designs are
all possible; morphology, jurisdiction, correction, and consequence decide.

François Bondy retrospectively attributed to Ignazio Silone the warning that
future Fascism would not announce itself as Fascism but would say “I am
anti-Fascism” ([Bondy, *European Notebook*, recollecting a 1945 oral
remark](https://books.google.de/books?id=CPdKDwAAQBAJ&pg=PT250)). The source must
be cited as Bondy's recollection rather than a surviving contemporaneous Silone
text. Its licensed use here is narrow: **anti-fascist self-description cannot
acquit a formation, and the name alone cannot convict it.**

The phrase ***whole-of-society approach*** is a particularly important active
vocabulary lead, but not a dictionary synonym for *Gleichschaltung*. It was in
ordinary institutional use at least by the World Health Organization's 2009
[whole-of-society pandemic-readiness guidance](https://www.who.int/publications/i/item/whole-of-society-pandemic-readiness),
which coordinated public and private organizations and essential services while
also invoking ethical norms and human rights. NATO currently uses the phrase for
[cooperation among government, the private sector, and civil society](https://nato.int/en/what-we-do/deterrence-and-defence/resilience-civil-preparedness-and-article-3)
in resilience against disasters, attacks, and hybrid threats. Those uses establish
both the phrase's broad cross-domain reach and its possible bounded, plural,
function-specific meaning. They also defeat a simple claim that the term itself
was invented in 2010 or necessarily denotes coercive alignment.
Likewise, OECD transition-finance guidance uses
[*whole-of-economy approach*](https://www.oecd.org/en/publications/oecd-guidance-on-transition-finance_7c68a1ee-en/full-report/component-7.html)
for cross-sector coverage and enabling policy. The phrase establishes reach; its
governance topology and coercive character remain separate questions.

These examples are evidence about vocabulary and possible institutional forms,
not character references for the organizations using them. Under the
**institutional-symmetry and scale-capacity rule**, **no organization is above
the typed gates**: WHO, NATO, the UN, national governments, firms, NGOs, and
informal networks must be judged by the same operative relations and consequences. Indeed,
an international organization may already possess a large **systemic skeleton**:
mandates, member-state interfaces, funding, expert and implementation networks,
standards, data channels, logistical capacity, and recognized communication
authority. If such a skeleton is used for cross-domain alignment, its
cross-jurisdiction population exposure and potential blast radius can be much
greater than those of a single polity. Vigilance, disclosure, independent review,
reversibility, refusal or exit where feasible, and remedy must therefore scale
with installed capability and reach. This is a **conditional severity rule**, not
guilt by size: scale raises what an evidenced misuse could do; it does not prove
that misuse, *Gleichschaltung*, Fascism, or hybrid war has occurred.

At the same time, the phrase can name exactly the scale and institutional surface
upon which a contemporary *Gleichschaltung* attempt would operate. The crosswalk
therefore translates **operative roles**, not words:

| Historical or operative role | Current vocabulary leads to inspect | Operation-level question | Boundary against lexical conviction |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Encompassing alignment process** | *Whole-of-society*, *whole-of-government*, *whole-of-economy*, societal mobilization, policy coherence, resilience, or alignment | Are multiple independent domains being made answerable to one privileged ideological direction, with refusal and correction constricted? | Scope language can describe coordination only. Narrow purpose, plural authority, preserved independence, expiry, appeal, and effective refusal defeat the *Gleichschaltung* gate. |
| **The *fascio/fascis* bundle at the economic plane** | Stakeholder capitalism, public-private cooperation or partnership, multistakeholder governance, social partnership, or coordinated capitalism | Are capital, labour, government, civil-society, and expert bodies continuously bundled so that publicly or quasi-publicly selected purposes override market and associational correction? | Consultation, bargaining, regulation, and mixed economies are not automatically corporatism, *Gleichschaltung*, or Fascism. Test representation, binding force, independence, competition, correction, and exit. |
| **Private execution of public or sovereign-like functions** | Public-private partnership, outsourcing, implementation partner, NGO delivery, contractor or platform enforcement, co-regulation, private standard-setting, trusted-flagger or compliance network | Does public authority define, finance, induce, coordinate, privilege, or materially rely upon a private carrier to impose a burden, gate, classification, surveillance, exclusion, sanction, or service condition? Would direct public action face stronger legality, rights, transparency, record, due-process, or appeal constraints? | Procurement, grant funding, consultation, or independent private action is insufficient. Distinguish ordinary service delivery, formal *Beleihung*, state-controlled private form, induced private conduct, and genuinely independent action; legal attribution is jurisdiction-specific. |
| **Corporative organs and authorized intermediaries** | Stakeholder councils, platforms, action groups, partnerships, standard-setters, or representative forums | Who selects the represented categories and representatives; who sets their mandate; may outsiders compete; and are decisions advisory, contractual, regulatory, or practically compulsory? | A forum is not an organ of compulsory incorporation merely because government and firms attend it. |
| **Economy directed toward declared collective ends** | ESG frameworks, sustainable-finance taxonomies, transition plans, SDG alignment, common metrics, disclosure frameworks, or purpose-led management | Who defines the ends and metrics, who controls finance and permissions through them, whether firms co-design the rules governing rivals, and whether voters, owners, workers, customers, entrants, and courts can correct them | The merits of climate, social, governance, health, or security objectives neither establish nor defeat the topology. “Whole-of-economy” first describes coverage; it becomes corporatist or alignative only through evidenced governance relations. |
| **Population rendered an operational surface** | Human terrain, societal resilience, behavioural or cultural expertise, public engagement, or strategic communication | Are persons being informed and invited to cooperate, or modeled as manipulable terrain whose independent judgment, association, and dissent are obstacles to be neutralized? | Social research, public communication, and preparedness do not by themselves establish war, manipulation, or domestic targeting. |
| **Back-stage enforcement through ordinary interfaces** | Weinstein's *back-of-house retaliation* (BOHR), administrative coordination, platform or employer enforcement, credential, finance, procurement, data, access, and compliance systems | Do seemingly separate organizations route surveillance, exclusion, retaliation, or resource denial toward the same direction while leaving no effective independent front of house? | A common vendor, safety process, moderation decision, refusal of service, or regulatory duty does not prove a coordinated retaliation system. Trace the concrete route, authority, trigger, target, remedy, and recurrence. |
| **Aligned institutional centre versus excluded remainder** | Weinstein's *Wh-o-S* versus *B-R-o-S* cleavage; claims that dissenters are backward, benighted, irresponsible, disinformation-bearing, or enemy collaborators | Does disagreement remain legitimate inside the polity, or does the coordinated centre convert dissenting citizens into an operationally suspect remainder whose institutions must be neutralized? | Sharp rhetoric or social disagreement alone does not establish institutional exclusion, enemy treatment, or hybrid war. |

<a id="public-private-constitutional-proxy-hazard"></a>

###### Public-private constitutional proxies and organizational dark matter {#public-private-constitutional-proxy-hazard}

Public-private partnership is not inherently an evasion mechanism. Governments
may legitimately buy goods, contract for services, fund independent civil
society, establish reviewable co-regulation, or formally entrust a private person
with a public function. The hazard begins where **the function and practical
power move across the boundary but the public duties do not follow them**.

The German distinction is useful. *Hoheitliche Aufgaben*, the exercise of
*hoheitsrechtliche Befugnisse*, ordinary procurement, subsidy, and formal
***Beleihung*** are not synonyms. [Article 33(4) of the Basic
Law](https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/gg/art_33.html) provides that the exercise
of sovereign powers as a permanent task is, as a rule, to be entrusted to members
of the public service in a public-law relationship of service and loyalty.
Article 1(3) binds legislature, executive, and judiciary to fundamental rights.
In its Fraport judgment, the Federal Constitutional Court held that a
state-controlled mixed enterprise organized under private law remains directly
bound by fundamental rights: private legal form cannot supply a ***Flucht ins
Privatrecht*** ([BVerfG, 1 BvR 699/06](https://www.bundesverfassungsgericht.de/SharedDocs/Entscheidungen/DE/2011/02/rs20110222_1bvr069906.html)).
These authorities establish a German constitutional boundary, not a universal
rule that every publicly funded NGO, contractor, platform, or commercial carrier
is a state actor. Ownership, control, statutory delegation, *Beleihung*, coercion,
inducement, practical dependence, and legal remedy must be tested under the
applicable jurisdiction.

The candidate pathway is:

```text
public authority defines, funds, requests, pressures, or privileges an objective
        ↓
NGO, contractor, platform, association, or commercial carrier executes the gate
        ↓
the carrier invokes private discretion; government invokes organizational distance
        ↓
the affected person encounters a public-purpose burden without an intact
public-law record, reason-giving duty, equality constraint, review, or appeal route
        ↓
principal, funder, designer, contractor, standard, algorithm, and local executor
each own only a fragment of the decision
        ↓
unassigned responsibility accumulates as organizational dark matter
```

Audit the complete chain:

1. **Function:** What public or sovereign-like task, burden, classification,
   service, permission, exclusion, sanction, surveillance, or adjudication is the
   carrier actually performing? Does it merely supply inputs, or decide what
   happens to persons?
2. **Public nexus:** Which statute, contract, grant, procurement term, official
   request, threat, privileged data access, legal immunity, regulator signal,
   personnel interchange, coordination forum, or recurrent dependency connects
   the public actor to that conduct?
3. **Constraint delta:** Which rule would constrain direct public action—legal
   authority, equality, viewpoint neutrality, proportionality, notice,
   reason-giving, records, disclosure, hearing, judicial review, or remedy—and is
   it weaker at the private interface?
4. **Effective control and benefit:** Who defines success, supplies leverage,
   changes the carrier's incentives, can halt or reverse the operation, and
   receives the policy benefit? Formal independence does not answer these
   questions, but funding alone does not answer them either.
5. **Responsibility continuity:** Can an affected person identify the decision
   owner, obtain the operative rule and record, challenge the factual basis,
   reach an independent reviewer, secure correction, and obtain repair? If every
   node points elsewhere, the topology has generated a responsibility gap.
6. **Counterfactual:** Would the carrier have performed materially the same act,
   under materially the same rule and timing, without the public nexus? Could the
   government lawfully and constitutionally have performed it directly?

The **public-private constitutional-proxy hazard** is an observable topology; it
can exist through poor design, path dependence, sincere but fragmented
coordination, or institutional emergence. Classify **constitutional evasion by
proxy** only with stronger evidence that a competent public actor selected,
maintained, pressured, or knowingly exploited the arrangement to obtain the
constraint delta. The legal conclusion that the private conduct is attributable
to the state remains a separate jurisdiction-specific question.

Venkatesh Rao's source term **organizational dark matter** comes from [*The
Gervais Principle V: Heads I Win, Tails You
Lose*](https://ribbonfarm.com/2011/10/14/the-gervais-principle-v-heads-i-win-tails-you-lose/).
There it names the unaccounted-for residue of organizational blame after credit
and culpability have been partitioned among persons, culture, and bureaucracy;
the accumulating residue becomes visible indirectly through declining morale,
incompetence, Byzantine processes, error, and clogged exceptions. The present
public-private application is a Culture Mechanics extension from one
organization to a cross-organizational principal--carrier chain. It does not mean
a secret organization, hidden controller, or literally unobservable matter.

Rao's [***Powertalk***](https://ribbonfarm.com/2009/11/11/the-gervais-principle-ii-posturetalk-powertalk-babytalk-and-gametalk/)
is adjacent but distinct: layered, consequential communication in which the
power relation shifts while much of the exchange retains plausible deniability.
Use it only where evidence shows public and private actors negotiating leverage,
exchanging reality-information, signalling the intended outcome, or preserving
off-record deniability across the boundary. A contract, grant, meeting, policy
agreement, euphemism, or coordination forum alone establishes neither Powertalk
nor deliberate constitutional evasion.

This proxy pathway cuts across the classification ladder below. It can occur in
one outsourced service without corporatism or *Gleichschaltung*; it can also
supply carrying structure, BOHR-style enforcement, corporatist intermediation,
or a system plane of broader alignment. None of those stronger classifications
follows from outsourcing or organizational dark matter alone.

The crosswalk is intentionally a ladder rather than the chain
`whole-of-society = corporatism = Gleichschaltung = Fascism = hybrid war`:

| Finding | Minimum additional evidence |
|---|---|
| **Ordinary whole-of-society or whole-of-economy coordination** | A bounded common problem, disclosed authority, preserved independent institutions, review, correction, refusal where feasible, and a stopping condition |
| **Corporatist political-economic topology** | Structured and continuing intermediation among organized economic or social bodies and political authority, with substantial collective-purpose direction beyond ordinary market exchange; centralization, compulsion, democratic corrigibility, and subsidiarity remain further variables |
| **Polity-scale *Gleichschaltung* hazard** | The full process gate above: polity ambition, cross-domain alignment, privileged ideological orientation, loss of independent orientation, and constricted plural correction across the human, symbolic, and systemic planes |
| **Fascism** | The separate complete conjunction: strong whole-polity mechanical-solidarity morphology, jealousy, subjecthood absorption, and a directing function claiming exclusive authority to incarnate, interpret, and act for the collective self |
| **Hybrid-war operation** | A specified actor or functional command relation, adversarial political objective, target population or institutions, operational means, and evidence that the conduct is being used as warfare; social coordination or a *Gleichschaltung* morphology alone does not establish this strategy attribution |

Eric Weinstein's [ARC 2025 talk, “You're in a War (and You Don't Even Know
It)”](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mbdJtrXYBZs), as represented by the
transcription supplied for this project, proposes the sharper source-specific
mapping: hybrid war supplies the conflict frame, *human terrain* treats persons
and culture as an operational surface, *whole-of-society* aligns institutions,
and BOHR uses their backstage interfaces against the excluded remainder. He
calls the resulting institutional pattern “soft fascism.” Frank Hoffman's
[2007 *Conflict in the 21st Century: The Rise of Hybrid
Wars*](https://www.potomacinstitute.us/images/stories/publications/potomac_hybridwar_0108.pdf)
supports the named hybrid-war source bridge; United States Army accounts date
the Human Terrain System proof of concept to 2006 and first team deployment to
2007 ([Army historical account](https://www.army.mil/article/39608/human_terrain_teams_train_at_fort_leavenworth)).
They do not independently establish Weinstein's domestic-war classification or
one coordinated present-day operator. *Wh-o-S*, *B-R-o-S*, BOHR, and the
“no-name revolution” therefore remain Weinstein-specific warning vocabulary,
not new canonical population classes or established facts about every institution
he names.

The supplied [Malone article on the WEF and
corporatism](https://www.malone.news/p/penetrating-the-cabinets) makes a related
economic-topology claim. It maps the older coordination of capital, labour, and
political authority into stakeholder capitalism, public-private partnerships,
multistakeholder governance, sustainable-finance taxonomies, and ESG frameworks.
Official WEF material confirms its own language of [public-private cooperation
and stakeholder capitalism](https://www.weforum.org/press/2020/01/stakeholder-capitalism-a-manifesto-for-a-cohesive-and-sustainable-world/)
and its effort to produce [common ESG metrics aligned with the
SDGs](https://www.weforum.org/publications/measuring-stakeholder-capitalism-towards-common-metrics-and-consistent-reporting-of-sustainable-value-creation/).
Those sources establish vocabulary, convening, and proposed coordination; they
do not by themselves establish practical compulsion, loss of independent
orientation, *Gleichschaltung*, completed Fascism, or hybrid war.

Malone explicitly calls his comparison structural rather than ideological and,
using a conventional historical definition, denies that the WEF is Fascist. His
structural corporatism hypothesis is a useful lead; neither that hypothesis nor
his conventional negative classification settles the project's cross-doctrinal
functional gate. A named contemporary formation must be audited at a specified
period through actual governance, finance, appointments, standards, sanctions,
symbols, correction, refusal, exit, and consequences. Genealogical descent is
neither necessary nor sufficient: the “same spirit” means the same evidenced
organizing operation, not merely inherited words, shared personnel, or moral
analogy.

### 5.2 Culture-Embedded Strategy

**Culture-Embedded Strategy** is the narrower subtype for which the legacy
diagram used `Kulturstrategie`.

Examples proposed for study include:

- duty to work;
- punctuality and temporal discipline;
- craft honour and refusal to release defective work;
- truthfulness and honest reporting;
- maintenance before visible failure;
- reciprocal courtesy and turn-taking;
- safety as a non-negotiable professional obligation;
- rational-legal procedure, evidence, citation, and due process;
- stewardship of common infrastructure;
- management practices that align activities and reduce wasted effort.

These practices are not merely “socially valuable.” Their repeated operation can
change:

- output and productivity;
- defect and accident rates;
- transaction and monitoring costs;
- schedule reliability;
- trust and voluntary coordination;
- capacity accumulation;
- reserves, buffers, and resilience against catastrophe;
- the feasible range of individual life options.

The originating claim is materially strong:

> **A culture with a better motor of material production is less exposed to
> famine and can build more buffers against unforeseen crisis.**

**Epistemic status:** mechanism hypothesis and comparative-civilizational
research program. It is plausible that productive capability changes material
resilience, but the effect size, causal direction, distribution, external costs,
and boundary conditions require case-specific evidence.

### 5.3 Activity fit and compound returns

Culture-Embedded Strategies can operate like compound interest:

```text
small improvement in one repeated activity
+ fit with reinforcing activities
+ reduced friction and error
+ retention and reinvestment of gains
→ increasing capability across many cycles
```

Michael Porter's first-, second-, and third-order activity fit and Jim Collins's
flywheel supply external source leads. The Culture Mechanics synthesis is that
Systems Leadership matters because it helps install such a causal strategy across
an organization through leadership behaviour, systems, symbols, mythology,
habits, rewards, sanctions, and repeated experience.

“Compound interest” is an analogy, not a fixed exponential law. Gains can
diminish, reverse, be captured, or impose costs elsewhere. A successful outcome
also does not by itself identify which claimed mechanism produced it.

The legacy fragments proposed several large calibration cases:

- Cold War comparison between Western market economies and Eastern socialist
  economies;
- Deng Xiaoping's reforms as a wager that allowing some people to become wealthy
  would stimulate wider development;
- Spanish bullion versus English wealth-through-work orientations around the
  sixteenth century;
- Fredmund Malik's claim that management is a central societal function and the
  stronger author inference that long-run management differences help explain
  continental differences in living standards.

These are application and causal hypotheses, not clean experiments. War,
institutions, colonial history, geography, technology, selection, state capacity,
trade, capital, and measurement must be compared before they are treated as
evidence for one cultural mechanism.

### 5.4 Acting before the dice have fallen

In business and politics, decision-makers often cannot wait for decisive evidence.
They must act on a model, hunch, or partial signal while the difference between a
valuable Culture Strategy and a Culture Hallucination is not yet visible.

A reality-answerable experiment under such conditions should, where possible,
specify:

- the causal mechanism;
- assumptions and rival explanations;
- leading indicators;
- gate criteria;
- a timebox;
- stop, revise, and scale conditions;
- exposure limits and reversibility;
- who may interpret ambiguous results;
- which failure cannot be explained away.

Prefigurative local trials—living and organizing now in the proposed future
form—can test parts of a mechanism before society-wide adoption. Small trials do
not automatically predict scale; they can nevertheless expose missing causal
links and failure modes.

## 6. Culture as a mixed operative braid

The legacy diagram placed **Culture** downstream of both strategy and ideology.
Its meaning was compositional:

```text
Pure-Ideology components
  identity, transcendence, inherited loyalty,
  sacredness, symbols, affective attachment
                         \
                          \
                           → one operative CULTURAL BRAID
                          /
                         /
Culture-Strategic components
  productive sublimations, work ethics,
  coordination norms, management practice,
  materially generative institutions
```

Both sides may carry meaning, identity, sacredness, warmth, joy, duty, and
*Beseelung*. The diagram must not imply that feeling comes from one side and
material efficacy from the other.

### 6.1 Composition is not a second bigOther

Culture is mixed at the package level but unitary at the operative lens level.
The installed weave can make a professional duty sacred, reinterpret a religious
virtue as economic conduct, or bind local identity to a lifted constitutional
rule. Components do not retain an independent runtime meaning outside the weave
that makes them mutually intelligible.

### 6.2 Culture Program Complex versus composite object

A **Culture Program Complex** is a large Culture Program whose internal packages
are mutually fitted. A **composite culture-mechanical object** remains broader:
it may combine programs, institutions, carriers, fields, outputs, and processes
across analytical levels.

Do not infer:

```text
many components
→ many local bigOthers
```

The theory instead proposes:

```text
compatible components
→ one braided operative lens
→ one local bigOther execution
```

## 7. Independent dimensions

The branch distinction is one dimension among several. None of the following may
be silently inferred from another.

| Dimension | Question |
|---|---|
| **Branch** | Does accessible reality have the final right to defeat the load-bearing wager? |
| **Ideology classifier** | Does a sufficiently installed generator recurrently produce authorized social un-reality and recruit un-reason to defend its operative authority? |
| **Morphological form** | Does the complete Fascism conjunction or another specified form organize the carrier formation? |
| **Order level** | Does shared binding force reside in concrete answers or in the generator that produces and corrects them? |
| **Slot occupancy** | Is the one local bigOther stably occupied? Stable first-order and lifted programs both imply full capture. |
| **Person--programme form** | Is the programme lived predominantly as bounded enrichment, shared belonging, mission, or a sovereign Will that uses the person? |
| **Outward propagation and enforcement profile** | Does adherence spread through personal discovery, attraction, recruitment, formation, retention pressure, institutional propagation, coercion, violence, or another evidenced route? |
| **Corrigibility** | Can claims, classifications, leaders, and outputs be corrected or replaced? |
| **Jurisdiction** | What persons, domains, offices, institutions, and decisions does the program claim authority over? |
| ***Beseelung* intensity** | How affectively alive and conduct-directing is the program? |
| **Actuation profile** | Through which affects and motives—pressure, duty, aspiration, belonging, fear, grievance, joy, play, hope, or others—does the programme move carriers? |
| **Carrier topology and animation distribution** | Through which institutional, communal, professional, networked, or anonymous structures does the programme live, and where is intense animation concentrated within them? |
| **Receiver state and inherited capacity** | Which acute, chronic, developmental, scale, and inherited-capability conditions shape reception without determining it? |
| **Jealousy** | How strongly does it reject rival attachments or divided allegiance? |
| **Entrusted power** | What institutional capacity and coercive authority has it acquired? |
| **Capture susceptibility** | How readily can destructive actors or descendants seize its machinery? |
| **Hazard** | What scale, severity, recurrence, persistence, and reversibility characterize its offspring? |
| **Cultural interoperability** | Can differentiated orders translate meanings and coordinate common action above the anti-Babel floor? |
| **Subjecthood absorption** | What standing do personal judgment, conscience, property, relationship, refusal, and exit retain? |
| **Programmatic content profile** | Which referents, groups, goods, causal stories, distributive rules, custodians, and corrective practices organize the programme? |
| **Sacred-principal allocation audit** | Who or what receives ultimate standing, and who or what becomes its instrument? This remains an audit, not an accepted third constitutional orientation. |

### 7.1 Four person--programme forms and the outward propagation profile

The canonical forms are defined in
[*Culture Mechanics*](0010-culture-mechanics.semantic-definition.md#beseelung-and-four-propagation-and-enforcement-forms):

```text
private
→ collective
→ ideological form
→ fundamentalist form
```

They are not necessary historical stages. A program can skip, retreat, mix, or
place different carrier strata in different forms across institutions.

- **Private form:** the programme is a bounded enrichment within a larger life.
- **Collective form:** the programme is a shared belonging and source of mutual
  recognition.
- **Ideological form:** the programme becomes a mission the carrier should
  advance.
- **Fundamentalist form:** the programme is treated as a sovereign Will whose
  supposedly known demands use the carrier as an execution surface.

The outward profile is separately recorded as personal discovery, attraction,
recruitment, formation, retention pressure, institutional propagation, coercion,
or violence.
These operations often correlate with the four inward forms, but they neither
define nor prove them.
The “salt in the soup” private relation can be intensely *beseelt* while bounded;
a lone carrier can occupy a mission or sovereign-Will relation; and cool
functionaries can execute coercive enforcement without personally occupying the
fundamentalist form.

The original burden intuition remains valid as a separate constitutional rule:
scrutiny should rise as any programme moves from personal practice toward
collective organization, institutional control, coercive jurisdiction, and
irreversible authority.
That power-and-enforcement scale is not the definition of the four forms.

<a id="fanatic-offspring-as-an-indicator"></a>

### 7.2 Fanatic offspring as an indicator {#fanatic-offspring-as-an-indicator}

The originating diagnostic defines **fanatics** by extreme discharge:

- violence in service of the program, especially fatal violence against
  innocents; or
- extraordinary dedication of time, effort, resources, or life to its demands.

Fanaticism is evidence about *Beseelung* intensity, jealousy, progeny, and
possible malfunction. It is not the definition of Pure-Ideology and does not mean
that every carrier is fanatical.

#### Žižek bridge: perverse instrumentality, fundamentalist form, and fanatic discharge

Fanaticism identifies an observable intensity or discharge; it does not by
itself explain the actor's relation to authorization. One candidate mechanism is
the **perverse instrumentality** recovered from Slavoj Žižek's reading of Lacan
in [*How to Read Lacan*](https://wwnorton.co.uk/books/9780393329551-how-to-read-lacan) and defined within [the drive
orientation](0030-epistemic-constitution.semantic-definition.md#life-drive-death-drive-constitutional-orientation).

Žižek's source sequence is:

```text
the actor claims direct access to God, History, or another big Other
→ belief is converted into supposedly direct knowledge of its Will
→ ambiguity and subjective division are displaced onto the opponent
→ the actor presents themselves as the Will's instrument
→ the act is experienced as execution of necessity rather than an answerable
  personal choice
→ sacrifice or death may be treated as proof of truth and fidelity
```

His analysis of Mohammad Bouyeri's letter to Ayaan Hirsi Ali illustrates the
last transition: readiness to die for truth becomes the wish for death as proof
of truthfulness, and death is made the final separator of Truth from Lies.
Žižek's deliberately cross-partisan examples of the more general structure are
Osama bin Laden and President George W. Bush insofar as each presents political
action as directly ordered by divine will. He then associates religious
fundamentalism with a perverse libidinal economy in which the fundamentalist
“does not believe, he knows it directly.” By contrast, Sophia Karpai's refusal
to falsify medical evidence for Stalinist necessity is presented as the ethical
act opposed to elevation of oneself into the big Other's instrument.

Žižek's *fundamentalist* principally names an epistemic and libidinal posture in
this passage; the project's **fundamentalist form** names a person--programme
relation in which the programme is treated as a sovereign Will and the carrier
as its instrument. A carrier can exhibit Žižek's claimed direct-knowledge
posture without controlling coercive enforcement, and a programme can transmit
a fundamentalist relation through an institution without establishing that
every executor occupies Žižek's perverse structure.

Culture Mechanics separates five analytical objects:

| Object | Type | Governing question |
|---|---|---|
| **Perverse instrumentality** | Candidate subject--authorization mechanism | Does the actor claim direct knowledge of the big Other's Will and present the act as its exculpated execution? |
| **Fundamentalist form** | Person--programme form | Is the programme treated as a sovereign Will whose supposedly known demand displaces the carrier's answerable authorship? |
| **Coercive enforcement** | Outward propagation and enforcement operation | Does the programme currently authorize coercive, eliminative, or violent enforcement? |
| **Fanatic offspring** | Observable programme-output indicator | Does a carrier discharge extraordinary dedication, sacrifice, or violence in service of the programme? |
| **Ethical act under extreme cost** | Rival responsibility pathway | Does the person assume an answerable judgment against the claimed necessity of the big Other, as in Žižek's Karpai example? |

This decomposition increases diagnostic resolution. Extreme cost is an intensity
sensor, not a drive-valence or perversion test. Costly persistence can express
ethical responsibility, ordinary courage, strategic calculation, coerced
conduct, desperation, identity defence, or perverse instrumentality. The
discriminating questions are:

1. Is there an authenticated claim of direct divine, historical, scientific, or
   ideological authorization?
2. Is the source treated as unambiguous knowledge rather than a belief,
   interpretation, wager, or corrigible judgment?
3. Does the actor describe themselves as an instrument, executor, servant, or
   necessary vehicle whose personal authorship disappears?
4. Is doubt, contradiction, or division acknowledged in the self, or assigned
   wholly to a lying, cowardly, impure, or inconsistent opponent?
5. Does sacrifice serve an independent good, or is willingness to sacrifice
   itself offered as proof of truth, purity, or authorization?
6. Do authoritative texts, leaders, institutions, recruitment, training,
   celebration, and recurrence support the proposed programme-level relation?
7. What rival mechanism—coercion, grievance, strategic signalling, status,
   mental disorder, personal pathology, ordinary duty, or ethical
   responsibility—fits the same conduct?

The source relationship is therefore bounded:

```text
perverse instrumentality
may help generate or intensify
fundamentalist person--programme relation, coercive enforcement,
and fanatic discharge

but

fanatic discharge ≠ fundamentalist form
fundamentalist form ≠ perverse instrumentality
fanatic discharge ≠ perverse instrumentality
```

“The attacker was mentally ill” is not an automatic program-level exculpation.
Mental disorder, personal grievance, strategic organization, and cultural
programming can be joint causes. When a perpetrator seriously proclaims program
authorization, the Self-Attribution Presumption requires initial credence about
the perpetrator's own operative understanding and an immediate program-level
warning. It does not by itself prove parent-program causation. The subsequent
task is to compare scripts, targets, legitimation, recruitment, recurrence, base
rates, rival causes, and counterfactual exposure.

<a id="strong-programmatic-diversity"></a>

### 7.3 Strong Programmatic Diversity and the corrective-unity profile {#strong-programmatic-diversity}

The ordinary presence of different persons, views, skills, cultures, or
biological forms is not a Culture Program. Nor do anti-discrimination, open
association, minority protection, epistemic plurality, federalism, or a varied
workforce automatically instantiate the type below.

**Strong Programmatic Diversity** is a Culture Program or component satisfying
this conjunction:

1. **Selected group ontology:** specified identity or population categories are
   made institutionally legible and recurrently relevant.
2. **Constitutive pattern-good:** their recognition, representation, relation, or
   distribution is treated as a constitutive measure of institutional legitimacy,
   not merely a description or occasional instrument.
3. **Category-conscious operation:** the programme authorizes differentiated
   recognition, representation, opportunity, resource, burden, remedy, rule, or
   outcome correction in relation to those categories.
4. **Causal and distributive grammar:** a model explains why observed
   differences or disparities require the selected intervention and how benefit,
   burden, voice, office, or remedy should be allocated.
5. **Interpreter custody:** authorized actors define the relevant categories,
   baselines, indicators, exceptions, deficits, remedies, and completion or
   revision rules.
6. **Carrying structure:** institutions, roles, formation, records, incentives,
   budgets, sanctions, or professional practices reproduce the programme beyond
   one isolated decision.

“Strong” refers to programmatic and institutional depth, not moral validity,
coercive intensity, truth orientation, or drive direction. The profile can remain
inside a [nested relation to person-centred
Humanism](0020-epistemic-agency.semantic-definition.md#humanism-diversity-relations),
supplement it, or replace it. Replacement requires the additional
[pattern-principal
displacement](0040-mythology-construction.semantic-definition.md#pattern-principal-displacement)
in which the desired pattern can override the standing of its concrete bearers.
Strong Programmatic Diversity does not by itself establish Pure-Ideology,
compulsory supremacy, subjecthood absorption, un-reason, death-drive, capture,
*Machtpolitik*, *Willkürherrschaft*, or a Ragnarök trajectory.

#### Identity-differentiated corrective unity

The more specific **identity-differentiated corrective-unity profile** requires
all of the following:

```text
institutionally recognized identity groups
+ formal or universal equality judged insufficient under a structural or
  historical asymmetry account
+ deliberate promotion or representation of selected subordinate forms
+ asymmetric corrective obligation assigned to a dominant or majority group
+ authorized custodians define legitimate categories and correction
+ differentiated first-order forms remain subject to one compulsory
  higher-order ideological content
→ identity-differentiated corrective unity
```

The compact picture is:

> **Many differentiated forms, one authorized higher-order content.**

This strict profile supports the **functional-near-synonym hypothesis**:
Korenizatsiya, Maoist nationality policy, and a specified strong modern
Diversity programme may instantiate substantially the same abstract mechanism
despite different periods, institutions, territorial arrangements, and policy
instruments. The controlled historical comparison and source boundaries are in
the [cross-theory
panel](0050-culture-mechanics-analogies.semantic-inventory.md#functional-near-synonym-panel).

The classification is stronger than loose analogy only when evidence establishes
the complete conjunction in each case. It does not choose among direct
transmission, inheritance from a common intellectual source field, reconstruction
from shared premises, or independent functional convergence. It therefore
establishes no genealogy by itself. It also does not make every use of Diversity
communist, every category-conscious remedy an orthodoxy, the three histories
morally equivalent, or resistance to a programme proof of chauvinism, privilege,
or hostility to equality.

<a id="diversity-dividend-laundering"></a>

#### Diversity-dividend laundering {#diversity-dividend-laundering}

**Diversity-dividend laundering** is the transfer of credit earned by plural,
independent, competent viewpoints inside an integrative common order to visible
heterogeneity whose independence, competence, translation, shared reality, or
integration has not been demonstrated.

```text
genuinely different knowledge and criticism
+ common correction and synthesis procedure
→ possible superior integrated result
→ earned reputation of diversity

visible category or programme heterogeneity
→ reputation imported as if integration had already occurred
→ input represented as superior output
→ failure can be used to demand more of the same input
```

The mechanism matters because the classical `1 + 1 > 2` case depends upon more
than the presence of different inputs.
Contributors must bring relevant differences, retain enough independence to
correct one another, communicate across the interface, and participate in a
reality-answerable process capable of producing an alloy rather than mere
alternation, veto, dilution, or symbolic representation.

The audit compares claimed diversity with viewpoint independence, task-relevant
competence, common evidence, translation, decision quality, error discovery,
minority contribution, integration, and correction after failure.
It also asks whether the output was measured independently of the input category
and whether failure can ever reduce the programme's authorized scope.

The type does not imply that demographic difference is irrelevant, that
homogeneous groups perform better, or that every diversity programme launders a
dividend.
Visible heterogeneity can widen experience and discover blind spots.
Classification requires the semantic transfer from input to already-achieved
benefit together with missing or protected output evidence.
Its upstream semantic routes may include [projective
consensus](0040-mythology-construction.semantic-definition.md#projective-consensus-and-custodial-operationalization)
and master-signifier credit; its constitutional contrast is [democratic cultural
alloying](0040-mythology-construction.semantic-definition.md#democratic-cultural-alloying-and-integrative-interoperability).

<a id="carrier-topology-and-animation-distribution"></a>

### 7.4 Carrier topology and animation distribution {#carrier-topology-and-animation-distribution}

**Carrier topology** asks where a programme lives and through which recurring
relations it is transmitted, interpreted, financed, corrected, and succeeded.
It is independent of the private, collective, ideological, and fundamentalist
person--programme forms, which ask what position the programme occupies relative
to the person, rival programmes, and the surrounding world.

Ernst Troeltsch's church, sect, and mysticism types supply the central prior-art
warning: universal institutional administration, selective high-intensity
community, and inward or loosely networked authority are different social
architectures.
Culture Mechanics generalizes the axis without treating Troeltsch's historically
Christian types as exhaustive:

- universal or population-facing institutional administration;
- selective high-commitment community;
- inward, exemplary, or loosely networked authority;
- professional office and credentialed jurisdiction;
- associational, movement, or federated network;
- household and intergenerational transmission;
- market, media, platform, and audience topology; and
- episodic crowd or event-centred activation.

One programme can combine several topologies.
It can become ideologically propagative without ceasing to use a professional
office, or move from charismatic network to routinized institution without
changing its treatment of outsiders.
Conversely, two programmes can occupy similar organizational forms while
producing different person--programme relations and correction rules.

The topology must be crossed with **animation distribution**.
A programme can be weakly *beseelt* on average while a small, strategically
located upper tail of teachers, interpreters, appointing officers, organizers,
editors, donors, or exemplars maintains strong animation and transmits it through
institutional machinery to cooler executors.
Average survey intensity can therefore understate governing capacity.
The claim requires evidence about positions, gain, routing, selection,
succession, and conduct; hierarchy alone does not prove concentrated animation.

```text
carrier topology × person--programme form × actuation profile
× outward propagation and enforcement profile × animation distribution
→ ecology-specific operative configuration
```

No row determines truth, legitimacy, hazard, or an inevitable development path.
The matrix prevents “church,” “sect,” “network,” “profession,” “private,” and
“fundamentalist” from being used as interchangeable labels.

<a id="composite-carrier-ecology-and-renewal-cycle"></a>

#### Composite carrier ecology and renewal cycle {#composite-carrier-ecology-and-renewal-cycle}

One programme can distribute invention, intensification, administration, and
population reproduction across different carrier topologies:

```text
inward or loosely networked experiential innovators
→ selective high-Beseelung nucleus or cadre
→ professional interpreters
→ universal or population-facing institutional apparatus
→ low-intensity population-wide reproduction
```

The sequence is neither necessary nor one-way.
A routinized apparatus can lose animation; a selective reform nucleus can restore
intensity; an inward or charismatic experience can supply renewed authority; and
a successful movement can routinize into a successor apparatus.
Conversely, loss of central office can leave selective enclaves or loose networks
that preserve the programme.
Troeltsch's church, sect, and mysticism types supply the prior-art topology;
Culture Mechanics adds the composite lifecycle and its crossing with
person--programme form, *Beseelung* distribution, institutional amplification,
correction, and succession.

This topology does not make every intense nucleus a sect, every institution a
church, every inward experience mysticism, or every renewal beneficial.
Universal scale can require compromise and office; selective intensity can be
purchased by narrow membership and exit from wider responsibility.
Those are recurrent trade-offs to inspect, not fixed laws or moral rankings.

## 8. Ecological mechanics

### 8.1 Programs require carriers but are not exhausted by isolated carriers

Culture Programs run through people, symbols, institutions, media, practices, and
material arrangements. Networked local bigOthers can generate an emergent
symbolic-affective field that acts back upon participants. Ecology therefore
cannot be reduced to a census of independently formed private beliefs.

Relevant units include:

- claim;
- mythology;
- package;
- Culture Program Complex;
- carrier;
- organization;
- institution;
- descendant or offspring program;
- population;
- political order;
- cultural output.

Causal attribution fails when these units are silently exchanged.

<a id="cultural-self-maintenance-and-defence"></a>

#### Cultural self-maintenance and defence {#cultural-self-maintenance-and-defence}

An operative Culture Program does not persist by message repetition alone. Its
carriers, meanings, practices, institutions, and material arrangements perform a
distributed cluster of **cultural self-maintenance and defence** functions:

- transmit classifications, roles, skills, and permissions;
- preserve memory, precedent, exemplars, and succession;
- detect mismatch, criticism, rivalry, defection, or capture;
- explain, repair, reinterpret, or replace damaged scripts;
- regulate access, standing, membership, resources, and trusted channels;
- sanction violations and defend institutional embodiment;
- absorb compatible novelty while rejecting incompatible governing claims;
- return evidence and consequence to corrigible rules and authorities.

The cluster can be emergent, deliberately designed, or hybrid. A grown mythology,
formal office, informal reputation network, editorial convention, professional
standard, family practice, market selection process, or legal institution can
each perform part of it. No single immune centre, constructor, or command
hierarchy is required.

The functional category does not establish truth or legitimacy. At least four
profiles must be distinguished:

| Profile | Characteristic operation |
|---|---|
| **Adaptive defence** | Preserves valuable continuity while admitting evidence, bounded criticism, script repair, interpreter replacement, and succession |
| **Closed defence** | Treats disconfirming evidence, ordinary disagreement, or every rival as proof of hostility or corruption |
| **Captured defence** | Inherited legitimacy, offices, sanctions, or trusted channels begin protecting a successor program or substituted objective |
| **Autoimmune defence** | The program misclassifies persons, practices, institutions, or resources needed for its own stated purpose and viability as enemies |

The same act can occupy different profiles under different evidence. Excluding a
fabricated claim can be ordinary epistemic maintenance; suppressing a documented
finding can be closed defence; replacing a corrupt official can be adaptive
repair; purging independent correction can be capture. The classification
therefore requires the claimed threat, protected function, evidence path,
proportionality, correction route, and consequences.

The [systemic skeleton and living cultural
body](#systemic-skeleton-and-living-cultural-body) explain how these functions
become durable and animate. [Mythological apologetics and
polemics](0040-mythology-construction.semantic-definition.md#mythological-replacement-braiding-apologetics-and-polemics)
name important symbolic repair and conflict operations. Culture Program carrying
structure identifies the concrete finance, formation, communication, logistics,
protection, and succession substrate. None is interchangeable with the complete
self-maintenance-and-defence cluster.

The controlled **cultural immune-system analogy**, including adaptive, captured,
autoimmune, and deficient cases, belongs to [*Analogical Models in Culture
Mechanics*](0050-culture-mechanics-analogies.semantic-inventory.md#cultural-immune-system-extension).
Dissent is not disease, cultural difference is not a pathogen, and the
organismic image supplies no warrant for coercion.

<a id="receiver-state-and-inherited-capacity-ecology"></a>

#### Receiver-state and inherited-capacity ecology {#receiver-state-and-inherited-capacity-ecology}

“Orientation demand” is not one receiver condition.
Programme susceptibility and reproduction can change with temporal state,
social scale, inherited capacity, and institutional distance.
[Wilhelm Röpke's mass and social-crisis
analysis](https://cdn.mises.org/The%20Social%20Crisis%20of%20Our%20Time%20%281%29.pdf)
supplies prior art for distinguishing acute crowd activation from chronic mass
conditions, integration hunger, society-reference closure, decision-scale
displacement, intermediate pluralism, and consumption of inherited moral
preconditions.
Culture Mechanics separates those leads into testable relations rather than
adopting Röpke's complete Christian-humanist diagnosis or treating his preferred
human scale as a sufficient safeguard.
The ecology must distinguish:

- an **acute crowd**, in which proximity, common attention, event, and feedback
  produce short-lived activation;
- a **chronic mass condition**, in which isolation, uprooting, standardized
  mediation, dependency, or loss of intelligible place persists without one
  physical crowd;
- **integration hunger**, in which loneliness, boredom, disorientation, or loss
  of role increases demand for a programme able to make life socially legible;
- **belief-ground failure**, in which an inherited world ceases to support action
